  3374	/-- **Theorem 3 (clean route)**: a taut filling of a combinatorial 2-sphere is a *freely
  3375	clean-shellable* sticker ball, assembled
  3376	from the three discharged clean steps (`base_free_clean`, `deg3_step_clean`, `prime_step_clean`) via
  3377	the clean induction skeleton `theorem3_core_clean`.  Wrapped by the public endpoint
  3378	`taut_filling_is_anyrootedStickerball`. -/
  3379	theorem theorem3_clean {σ : Finset (Finset V)} {X M : Chain V} (hσ : IsSphere2 σ)
  3380	    (hU : UnitOn X σ) (hXc : bdry X = 0) (hMX : bdry M = X) (hT : IsTaut M)
  3381	    (hS : SimplicialChain M) : FreelyCleanShellable M.support σ :=
  3382	  theorem3_core_clean base_free_clean deg3_step_clean prime_step_clean hσ hU hXc hMX hT hS
  3383	
  3384	/-- **Theorem 2 (clean route)**: a taut filling of a combinatorial 2-sphere `σ` is a *clean ball*
  3385	(`IsCleanBall` = the paper's *stickerball* = a shellable clean ball: it admits a clean shelling).
  3386	
  3387	Two facts about this endpoint are worth making explicit (both are *named theorems*, not handwaves):
  3388	
  3389	* **It is genuinely clean, including connected vertex links.**  `IsCleanBall M.support σ` provably
  3390	  entails `Clean3Complex M.support` via `IsCleanBall.clean3Complex` — exported here as the standalone
  3391	  endpoint `taut_clean3Complex`.  `Clean3Complex = Pure3 ∧ TriangleBounded3 ∧ Normal3` and
  3392	  `Normal3 = EdgeLinkConnected ∧ VertexLinkConnected`, so connected vertex links (dim-0 links) and
  3393	  connected edge links (dim-1 links) — the paper's "normal" condition — are both delivered.  The
  3394	  per-step vertex-link compatibility is the `CleanGlueStep.hvlc` field, folded by
  3395	  `IsCleanShelling.clean3Complex`.
  3396	
  3397	* **The proof actually establishes the stronger free-start statement.**  `IsCleanBall` asks only for
  3398	  *one* clean shelling, but this theorem is a one-line weakening of `theorem3_clean`, which produces a
  3399	  clean shelling beginning with *any* prescribed tet (`FreelyCleanShellable`).  So the substance of
  3400	  Theorem 3 (free choice of starting tet) is already proved by the same minimal-counterexample
  3401	  induction; `theorem2_clean` simply forgets the head condition.  `IsCleanBall` (stickerball /
  3402	  shellable) and `FreelyCleanShellable` (free-start) are kept as *distinct* predicates on purpose —
  3403	  "stickerball" is not silently redefined to mean "freely shellable". -/
  3404	theorem theorem2_clean {σ : Finset (Finset V)} {X M : Chain V} (hσ : IsSphere2 σ)
  3405	    (hU : UnitOn X σ) (hXc : bdry X = 0) (hMX : bdry M = X) (hT : IsTaut M)
  3406	    (hS : SimplicialChain M) : IsCleanBall M.support σ := by
  3407	  obtain ⟨t, ht⟩ := aleph_base_support_nonempty hσ hU hMX
  3408	  obtain ⟨l, _, hlτ, hlnodup, hlshell⟩ := theorem3_clean hσ hU hXc hMX hT hS t ht
  3409	  exact ⟨l, hlτ, hlnodup, hlshell⟩
  3410	
  3411	/-- **Theorem 2, normality endpoint**: a taut filling of a combinatorial 2-sphere is a *clean
  3412	3-complex* — `Pure3 ∧ TriangleBounded3 ∧ Normal3`, where `Normal3 = EdgeLinkConnected ∧
  3413	VertexLinkConnected`.  This makes the paper's "normal" condition (connected vertex *and* edge links)
  3414	a first-class named conclusion rather than something a consumer must unpack from `IsCleanBall`.
  3415	In particular `(taut_clean3Complex …).2.2.2 : VertexLinkConnected M.support`. -/
  3416	theorem taut_clean3Complex {σ : Finset (Finset V)} {X M : Chain V} (hσ : IsSphere2 σ)
  3417	    (hU : UnitOn X σ) (hXc : bdry X = 0) (hMX : bdry M = X) (hT : IsTaut M)
  3418	    (hS : SimplicialChain M) : Clean3Complex M.support :=
  3419	  (theorem2_clean hσ hU hXc hMX hT hS).clean3Complex
  3420	
  3421	/-! ## Public endpoints (mathematically named)
  3422	
  3423	The primary, paper-independent statements.  A taut filling of a combinatorial 2-sphere is an
  3424	*anyrooted stickerball*; the weaker certificates are corollaries. -/
  3425	
  3426	/-- **Main theorem (Theorems 2+3 unified): a taut filling of a combinatorial 2-sphere is an anyrooted
  3427	stickerball.**  That is, its support is a shellable clean 3-complex with nonempty boundary
  3428	(`IsStickerball` — the combinatorial `B³` certificate) *and* a clean shelling can begin with any
  3429	prescribed tetrahedron (`FreelyCleanShellable`).  Subsumes both the ordinary stickerball certificate
  3430	(paper Theorem 2) and the free choice of starting tet (paper Theorem 3): the minimal-counterexample
  3431	induction proves the anyrooted statement directly (`theorem3_clean`), of which the existence form
  3432	(`theorem2_clean`) is the weakening. -/
  3433	theorem taut_filling_is_anyrootedStickerball {σ : Finset (Finset V)} {X M : Chain V}
  3434	    (hσ : IsSphere2 σ) (hU : UnitOn X σ) (hXc : bdry X = 0) (hMX : bdry M = X) (hT : IsTaut M)
  3435	    (hS : SimplicialChain M) : IsAnyrootedStickerball M.support σ :=
  3436	  ⟨⟨theorem2_clean hσ hU hXc hMX hT hS, hσ.nonempty⟩, theorem3_clean hσ hU hXc hMX hT hS⟩
  3437	
  3438	/-- A taut filling of a combinatorial 2-sphere is a *stickerball* (shellable clean ball with nonempty
  3439	boundary — the combinatorial `B³` certificate).  Corollary of `taut_filling_is_anyrootedStickerball`. -/
  3440	theorem taut_filling_is_stickerball {σ : Finset (Finset V)} {X M : Chain V} (hσ : IsSphere2 σ)
  3441	    (hU : UnitOn X σ) (hXc : bdry X = 0) (hMX : bdry M = X) (hT : IsTaut M)
  3442	    (hS : SimplicialChain M) : IsStickerball M.support σ :=

  3444	
  3445	/-- A taut filling of a combinatorial 2-sphere is a *clean 3-complex* — pure, every triangle in one or
  3446	two tets, and *both* links connected (`VertexLinkConnected` and `EdgeLinkConnected`). -/

   409	/-- **Theorem 1, splitting** (Doyle–Ellison–Wang): for `n ≥ 2`, ANY taut
   410	filling `M` of an almost disjoint union `X + Y` splits: filtering `M`
   411	along `· ⊆ A` yields taut fillings of `X` and of `Y` summing to `M`. -/
   412	theorem IsTaut.splits {A B : Finset V} {n : ℕ}
   413	    (hn : 2 ≤ n) {p q : V} (hp : p ∈ A ∩ B) (hq : q ∈ A ∩ B) (hpq : p ≠ q)
   414	    (hC : (A ∩ B).card ≤ n + 1)
   415	    {X Y : Chain V}
   416	    (hX : ∀ s ∈ X.support, s ⊆ A ∧ s.card = n + 1)
   417	    (hY : ∀ s ∈ Y.support, s ⊆ B ∧ s.card = n + 1)
   418	    (hXc : bdry X = 0) (hYc : bdry Y = 0)
   419	    {M : Chain V} (hMt : IsTaut M) (hM1 : bdry M = X + Y) :
   420	    bdry (M.filter (fun t => t ⊆ A)) = X ∧
   421	    bdry (M.filter (fun t => ¬ t ⊆ A)) = Y ∧
   422	    IsTaut (M.filter (fun t => t ⊆ A)) ∧

   497	
   498	/-- **Theorem 1, splitting (full paper statement)**: for `n ≥ 2`, the
   499	hypothesis `(A ∩ B).card ≤ n + 1` alone suffices — no witnessing pair
   500	`p ≠ q` is needed.  When `|A ∩ B| ≥ 2` we extract a pair and invoke
   501	`IsTaut.splits`; when `|A ∩ B| ≤ 1` we enlarge both sides by the same
   502	fresh vertices `F` to reach `|A' ∩ B'| = 2 ≤ n + 1`, run the split along
   503	`· ⊆ A'`, then transfer it back to `· ⊆ A`.  The transfer is valid because
   504	every generator of the filling `M` lies in `A ∪ B`, hence is disjoint from
   505	the fresh set `F`, so `t ⊆ A' ↔ t ⊆ A` on `M.support`.
   506	
   507	`[Infinite V]` only guarantees that fresh cut vertices are available for the
   508	`|A ∩ B| ≤ 1` enlargement; a taut filling never uses vertices beyond its boundary
   509	(`IsTaut.vert_subset`). -/
   510	theorem IsTaut.splits_full {V : Type*} [LinearOrder V] [Infinite V]
   511	    {A B : Finset V} {n : ℕ} (hn : 2 ≤ n) (hC : (A ∩ B).card ≤ n + 1)
   512	    {X Y : Chain V}
   513	    (hX : ∀ s ∈ X.support, s ⊆ A ∧ s.card = n + 1)
   514	    (hY : ∀ s ∈ Y.support, s ⊆ B ∧ s.card = n + 1)
   515	    (hXc : bdry X = 0) (hYc : bdry Y = 0)
   516	    {M : Chain V} (hMt : IsTaut M) (hM1 : bdry M = X + Y) :
   517	    bdry (M.filter (fun t => t ⊆ A)) = X ∧
   518	    bdry (M.filter (fun t => ¬ t ⊆ A)) = Y ∧
   519	    IsTaut (M.filter (fun t => t ⊆ A)) ∧
   520	    IsTaut (M.filter (fun t => ¬ t ⊆ A)) ∧
   521	    M.filter (fun t => t ⊆ A) + M.filter (fun t => ¬ t ⊆ A) = M := by
   522	  classical
   523	  by_cases h2 : 2 ≤ (A ∩ B).card
   524	  · obtain ⟨p, hp, q, hq, hpq⟩ := Finset.one_lt_card.mp h2
   525	    exact IsTaut.splits hn hp hq hpq hC hX hY hXc hYc hMt hM1
   526	  · push_neg at h2
   527	    have hn1 : 1 ≤ n := by omega
   528	    have hle : (A ∪ B).card ≤ (A ∪ B).card + (2 - (A ∩ B).card) := Nat.le_add_right _ _
   529	    obtain ⟨W, hWsub, hWcard⟩ :=
   530	      Infinite.exists_superset_card_eq (A ∪ B) _ hle
   531	    set F := W \ (A ∪ B) with hF
   532	    have hFdisj : Disjoint F (A ∪ B) := Finset.sdiff_disjoint
   533	    have hFcard : F.card = 2 - (A ∩ B).card := by
   534	      have hkey : F.card + (A ∪ B).card = (A ∪ B).card + (2 - (A ∩ B).card) := by
   535	        rw [hF, Finset.card_sdiff_add_card_eq_card hWsub, hWcard]
   536	      omega
   537	    set A' := A ∪ F with hA'
   538	    set B' := B ∪ F with hB'
   539	    have hinter : A' ∩ B' = (A ∩ B) ∪ F := by
   540	      ext x
   541	      simp only [hA', hB', Finset.mem_inter, Finset.mem_union]
   542	      constructor
   543	      · rintro ⟨hxA | hxF, hxB | hxF⟩
   544	        · exact Or.inl ⟨hxA, hxB⟩
   545	        · exact Or.inr hxF
   546	        · exact Or.inr hxF
   547	        · exact Or.inr hxF
   548	      · rintro (⟨hxA, hxB⟩ | hxF)
   549	        · exact ⟨Or.inl hxA, Or.inl hxB⟩
   550	        · exact ⟨Or.inr hxF, Or.inr hxF⟩
   551	    have hABFdisj : Disjoint (A ∩ B) F :=
   552	      (hFdisj.mono_right (Finset.inter_subset_left.trans Finset.subset_union_left)).symm
   553	    have hcard' : (A' ∩ B').card = 2 := by
   554	      rw [hinter, Finset.card_union_of_disjoint hABFdisj, hFcard]
   555	      omega
   556	    have h1lt : 1 < (A' ∩ B').card := by rw [hcard']; omega
   557	    obtain ⟨p, hp, q, hq, hpq⟩ := Finset.one_lt_card.mp h1lt

    48	/-- **The cut exists.** A non-face triangle's edge-cycle bounds a face-set `W`
    49	(this is exactly where H₁ = 0 is spent). -/
    50	theorem exists_cut {σ : Finset (Finset V)} (h : IsSphere2 σ) {γ : Finset V}
    51	    (hγ3 : γ.card = 3) (hγe : γ.powersetCard 2 ⊆ edgesOf σ) :
    52	    ∃ W : C2 σ, bd2 σ W = gammaChain σ γ := by
    53	  have hcyc : gammaChain σ γ ∈ LinearMap.ker (bd1 σ) :=
    54	    LinearMap.mem_ker.mpr (bd1_gammaChain hγ3 hγe)
    55	  rw [← range_bd2_eq_ker_bd1 h] at hcyc
    56	  exact LinearMap.mem_range.mp hcyc

    62	  (Finset.univ.filter (fun f : σ => W f = 1)).image Subtype.val

   530	/-! ### H₁ = 0 — every 1-cycle bounds -/
   531	
   532	/-- **The watershed.** On a combinatorial 2-sphere every 1-cycle is a boundary:
   533	`range ∂₂ = ker ∂₁`. Proof: `range ∂₂ ⊆ ker ∂₁` (from ∂∂=0), and both have
   534	finrank `F − 1` — `range ∂₂` by b₂=1, `ker ∂₁` by r₁=V−1 together with the given
   535	χ = 2 — so they coincide. -/
   536	theorem range_bd2_eq_ker_bd1 {σ : Finset (Finset V)} (h : IsSphere2 σ) :
   537	    LinearMap.range (bd2 σ) = LinearMap.ker (bd1 σ) := by
   538	  have hle : LinearMap.range (bd2 σ) ≤ LinearMap.ker (bd1 σ) :=

     1	import Taut.Ball
     2	import Taut.Pseudomanifold
     3	
     4	/-!
     5	# Clean shellability — the public, by-definition-simplicial shelling
     6	
     7	The **clean** layer threads the accumulated tet-set `τ` and forbids rogue
     8	lower-dimensional intersections: `CleanGlueStep.clean` says every face of the new
     9	tet already present in `τ` lies inside one of the shared boundary triangles.
    10	
    11	`CleanGlueStep.clean` is the correct no-rogue *shape* but does **not** by itself
    12	supply the quantitative compatibilities (`faceCount ≤ 1`, link attachment) that
    13	`clean3Complex_insert` needs.  So `CleanGlueStep` **carries them as extra fields**
    14	(`newTet`, `hpmc`, `helc`, `hvlc`).  This makes the clean shelling
    15	**self-certifying**: its prefixes are `Clean3Complex` with no further input
    16	(`CleanShellFrom.clean3Complex`, `IsCleanBall.clean3Complex`).
    17	
    18	The exported public predicates of Theorem 2/3 are these clean ones
    19	(`IsCleanBall`, `FreelyCleanShellable`).
    20	-/
    21	
    22	namespace Taut
    23	
    24	open Finset
    25	
    26	variable {V : Type*} [LinearOrder V]
    27	
    28	/-- One **clean** glue step.  Beyond the boundary glue (`weak`) and the no-rogue
    29	*shape* condition (`clean` — every face of `t` already in `τ` lies in a shared
    30	boundary triangle), it carries the *quantitative* clean-insert certificates that
    31	`clean3Complex_insert` consumes.  These do **not** follow from `clean` alone, so
    32	they are explicit fields. -/
    33	structure CleanGlueStep (t : Finset V) (τ B B' : Finset (Finset V)) : Prop where
    34	  /-- the underlying boundary glue step (one or two shared faces, symmetric difference). -/
    35	  weak : BoundaryGlueStep t B B'
    36	  /-- no rogue old face: every face of `t` already in `τ` lies in a shared boundary triangle. -/
    37	  clean : ∀ f, f ⊆ t → (∃ s ∈ τ, f ⊆ s) → ∃ g ∈ tetFaces t ∩ B, f ⊆ g
    38	  /-- `t` is fresh (rules out a doubled tet). -/
    39	  newTet : t ∉ τ
    40	  /-- each triangle of `t` lies in ≤ 1 old tet (so triangle-count stays ≤ 2). -/
    41	  hpmc : ∀ f, f.card = 3 → f ⊆ t → faceCount τ f ≤ 1
    42	  /-- each edge of `t` is new to `τ` or already shares an apex with `τ`'s edge-link there. -/
    43	  helc : ∀ e, e ⊆ t → e.card = 2 →
    44	    edgeLinkVerts τ e = ∅ ∨ ((t \ e) ∩ edgeLinkVerts τ e).Nonempty
    45	  /-- each vertex of `t` is new to `τ` or already shares an apex with `τ`'s vertex-link there. -/
    46	  hvlc : ∀ v ∈ t, vertexLinkVerts τ v = ∅ ∨
    47	    ((t \ {v}) ∩ vertexLinkVerts τ v).Nonempty
    48	
    49	/-- Clean shelling from boundary `B₀`, **threading the accumulated tet-set `τ`**:
    50	each step glues onto the set built so far, then recurses on `insert t τ`. -/
    51	def CleanShellFrom :
    52	    Finset (Finset V) → Finset (Finset V) → List (Finset V) → Finset (Finset V) → Prop
    53	  | _, B₀, [],     B => B = B₀
    54	  | τ, B₀, t :: l, B => ∃ B₁, CleanGlueStep t τ B₀ B₁ ∧ CleanShellFrom (insert t τ) B₁ l B
    55	
    56	/-- A clean shelling with final boundary `B`: the head tet is the initial
    57	tetrahedron (accumulated set `{t}`, boundary `tetFaces t`, no glue step), and the
    58	rest are glued on cleanly in order. -/
    59	def IsCleanShelling : List (Finset V) → Finset (Finset V) → Prop
    60	  | [],     _ => False
    61	  | t :: l, B => t.card = 4 ∧ CleanShellFrom {t} (tetFaces t) l B
    62	
    63	/-- A *clean ball*: a tet-set admitting a clean shelling (a nodup ordering of its
    64	tets gluing up cleanly to boundary `B`). -/
    65	def IsCleanBall (τ B : Finset (Finset V)) : Prop :=
    66	  ∃ l : List (Finset V), l.toFinset = τ ∧ l.Nodup ∧ IsCleanShelling l B
    67	
    68	/-- *Freely clean shellable*: **any** tet can serve as the head of a clean
    69	shelling.  This is the public free-sticker-ball predicate Theorem 3 exports. -/
    70	def FreelyCleanShellable (τ B : Finset (Finset V)) : Prop :=
    71	  ∀ t ∈ τ, ∃ l : List (Finset V),
    72	    l.head? = some t ∧ l.toFinset = τ ∧ l.Nodup ∧ IsCleanShelling l B
   118	The quantitative fields of `CleanGlueStep` feed `clean3Complex_insert`, so a clean
   119	shelling carries the manifold invariant with **no further chain input**. -/
   120	
   121	lemma CleanGlueStep.clean3Complex {t : Finset V} {τ B B' : Finset (Finset V)}
   122	    (hstep : CleanGlueStep t τ B B') (hτ : Clean3Complex τ) :
   123	    Clean3Complex (insert t τ) :=
   124	  clean3Complex_insert hstep.weak.card4 hstep.newTet hτ hstep.hpmc hstep.helc hstep.hvlc

   129	    {B : Finset (Finset V)} (hτ : Clean3Complex τ) (h : CleanShellFrom τ B₀ l B) :
   130	    Clean3Complex (τ ∪ l.toFinset) := by

/bin/zsh -lc "nl -ba lean/Taut/Ball.lean | sed -n '32,58p'" in /Users/doyle/Dropbox/taut
 succeeded in 0ms:
    32	
    33	variable {V : Type*} [LinearOrder V]
    34	
    35	/-- The four triangular faces of a tetrahedron `t` (the card-3 subsets). -/
    36	abbrev tetFaces (t : Finset V) : Finset (Finset V) := t.powersetCard 3
    37	
    38	/-- **Weak boundary-trace primitive** (the clean layer wraps it as
    39	`CleanGlueStep.weak`).  One gluing step of a shelling: tet `t` is glued onto a
    40	ball whose boundary is `B`, producing the ball with boundary `B'`.
    41	
    42	* `card4` — `t` is a tetrahedron (4 vertices);
    43	* `shared` — `t` meets the current boundary in **one or two** of its faces
    44	  (type 1 or 2; type 3 is excluded, as it would create an interior vertex);
    45	* `newBdry` — the new boundary is the symmetric difference of `B` and the
    46	  faces of `t`: shared faces become interior, the rest are exposed.
    47	
    48	It does not assert the new boundary is a 2-sphere (that holds, but is the separate,
    49	deferred fact `glueStep_preserves_isSphere2`, not needed for the main theorem). -/
    50	structure GlueStep (t : Finset V) (B B' : Finset (Finset V)) : Prop where
    51	  card4 : t.card = 4
    52	  shared : (tetFaces t ∩ B).card = 1 ∨ (tetFaces t ∩ B).card = 2
    53	  newBdry : B' = (B \ tetFaces t) ∪ (tetFaces t \ B)
    54	
    55	/-- One glue step, transported by erasing the interface face `γ` and adjoining the
    56	disjoint piece `K`, when the tet avoids both.  The one reassembly lemma still in
    57	use: `CleanGlueStep.erase_union_disjoint` lifts it to the clean layer (the degree-3

   135	      · rfl
   136	
   137	/-- A taut chain whose boundary is pure of dimension `k` is itself pure of
   138	dimension `k+1`: optimal fillings carry no junk. -/
   139	theorem IsTaut.dim_pure {M : Chain V} (hM : IsTaut M) {k : ℕ}
   140	    (hbd : ∀ s ∈ (bdry M).support, s.card = k) :
   141	    ∀ s ∈ M.support, s.card = k + 1 := by
   142	  set P := dimPart (k + 1) M with hP
   143	  have hPM : SubChain P M := subChain_filter _ M
   144	  have hbdP : bdry P = bdry M := by
   145	    rw [hP, ← dimPart_bdry, dimPart_eq_self hbd]
   146	  have h1 : Zvol (bdry M) ≤ nrm P := Zvol_le hbdP
   147	  have h2 := hPM.nrm_add_nrm_sub
   148	  rw [IsTaut] at hM
   149	  have h0 : nrm (M - P) = 0 := by omega
   150	  have hMP : M = P := sub_eq_zero.mp (nrm_eq_zero_iff.mp h0)
   151	  intro s hs
   152	  rw [hMP, hP] at hs
   153	  rw [dimPart, Finsupp.support_filter, Finset.mem_filter] at hs
   154	  exact hs.2
   155	
   156	/-- A taut chain in dimensions ≥ 1 has all its vertices on its boundary. -/
   157	theorem IsTaut.vert_subset {M : Chain V} (hM : IsTaut M)
   158	    (hdim : ∀ s ∈ M.support, 2 ≤ s.card) :
   159	    vert M ⊆ vert (bdry M) := by
   160	  intro x hx
   161	  by_contra hxB
   162	  exact hM.no_internal_vertex hdim hx hxB

Remaining discrepancies:

1. `notes/taut-lean-proof.tex:58-62`: MISSING-HYPOTHESIS.  
Corrected sentence: “For any chain `X` with a filling `W` satisfying `bdry W = X`, the infimum is attained, so `Zvol(X)=min{||M|| : bdry M=X}`; in particular this applies to cycles once a cone vertex is available.”

2. `notes/taut-lean-proof.tex:259-262`: WRONG-CITATION.  
Corrected sentence: “The identity `bdry M_A = X` follows by applying `Kmap A p` to `bdry M_A + bdry M_B = X + Y`; then `bdry M_B = Y` follows by cancellation. Tautness follows from `||M_A||+||M_B||=||M||`, additivity of `Zvol`, and `Zvol X ≤ ||M_A||`, `Zvol Y ≤ ||M_B||`.”
